```ts
import { competitions, lines } from "./types.ts";
import type { Pool } from "./types.ts";

// The same rules the database enforces, checked before anything is written,
// so a bad night fails in the job and not in a deploy.

const SLUG = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
const QID = /^Q\d+$/;
const ISO_DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const COUNTRY = /^[A-Z]{2}$/;
const year = (y: number | null) => y === null || (Number.isInteger(y) && y >= 1900 && y <= 2100);
const count = (n: number | null) => n === null || (Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0);

export function validatePool(input: unknown, governorateIds: Set<string>): string[] {
  const pool = input as Pool;
  if (
    pool?.version !== 1 ||
    !Array.isArray(pool.players) ||
    !Array.isArray(pool.clubs) ||
    !Array.isArray(pool.honours) ||
    !Array.isArray(pool.flags)
  ) {
    return ["not a version 1 pool"];
  }
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const clubIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const club of pool.clubs) {
    const at = `club ${club.id}`;
    if (!SLUG.test(club.id) || clubIds.has(club.id)) errors.push(`${at}: bad or duplicate id`);
    clubIds.add(club.id);
    if (!QID.test(club.wikidataId)) errors.push(`${at}: bad Wikidata id`);
    if (!club.nameLatin) errors.push(`${at}: no name`);
    if (!COUNTRY.test(club.country)) errors.push(`${at}: country ${club.country}`);
  }
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  const qids = new Set<string>();
  for (const p of pool.players) {
    const at = `player ${p.id}`;
    if (!SLUG.test(p.id)) errors.push(`${at}: id is not a slug`);
    if (p.clubId !== null && !clubIds.has(p.clubId)) errors.push(`${at}: unknown club ${p.clubId}`);
    if (p.birthCountry !== null && !COUNTRY.test(p.birthCountry)) errors.push(`${at}: birth country ${p.birthCountry}`);
    if (p.governorate !== null && !governorateIds.has(p.governorate)) errors.push(`${at}: unknown governorate ${p.governorate}`);
    if (p.governorate !== null && p.birthCountry !== null && p.birthCountry !== "TN") {
      errors.push(`${at}: governorate ${p.governorate} but born in ${p.birthCountry}`);
    }
    if (p.photo && (!p.photo.file || !p.photo.licence || !p.photo.sourceUrl)) errors.push(`${at}: photo without licence or source page`);
    if (ids.has(p.id)) errors.push(`${at}: duplicate id`);
    ids.add(p.id);
    if (!QID.test(p.wikidataId) || qids.has(p.wikidataId)) errors.push(`${at}: duplicate Wikidata id ${p.wikidataId}`);
    qids.add(p.wikidataId);
    if (!p.nameLatin) errors.push(`${at}: no Latin name`);
    if (!lines.includes(p.position)) errors.push(`${at}: position ${p.position}`);
    if (!ISO_DATE.test(p.birthDate)) errors.push(`${at}: birth date ${p.birthDate}`);
    if (!count(p.caps) || !count(p.goals)) errors.push(`${at}: caps or goals`);
    if (p.capsAsOf !== null && !ISO_DATE.test(p.capsAsOf)) errors.push(`${at}: caps date ${p.capsAsOf}`);
    for (const s of p.history) {
      if (s.clubId !== null && !clubIds.has(s.clubId)) errors.push(`${at}: spell at unknown club ${s.clubId}`);
      if (!year(s.from) || !year(s.to) || !count(s.apps) || !count(s.goals)) errors.push(`${at}: spell at ${s.clubName} has an impossible number`);
      if (s.from !== null && s.to !== null && s.from > s.to) errors.push(`${at}: spell at ${s.clubName} ends before it starts`);
    }
    if (!p.pools.active && !p.pools.legend) errors.push(`${at}: in neither pool`);
  }
  for (const h of pool.honours) {
    if (!competitions.includes(h.competition) || !clubIds.has(h.clubId) || !year(h.seasonStart)) {
      errors.push(`honour ${h.competition} ${h.seasonStart}: invalid`);
    }
  }
  return errors;
}
```

`src/pipeline/report.ts`:

```ts
import type { Competition, Pool, PoolHonour, PoolPlayer, SourceStatus } from "./types.ts";

export type CoverageRow = { field: string; count: number; total: number };

const MEASURES: [string, (p: PoolPlayer) => boolean][] = [
  ["Arabic name", (p) => p.nameArabic !== null],
  ["French name", (p) => p.nameFrench !== null],
  ["Photo", (p) => p.photo !== null],
  ["Governorate or born abroad", (p) => p.governorate !== null || (p.birthCountry !== null && p.birthCountry !== "TN")],
  ["Current club", (p) => p.clubId !== null],
  ["Caps with an as-of date", (p) => p.capsAsOf !== null],
  ["Three or more career spells", (p) => p.history.length >= 3],
  ["Detailed position", (p) => p.positionDetail !== null],
  ["English article", (p) => p.wiki.en !== null],
  ["French article", (p) => p.wiki.fr !== null],
];

export function computeCoverage(players: PoolPlayer[]): CoverageRow[] {
  return MEASURES.map(([field, has]) => ({ field, count: players.filter(has).length, total: players.length }));
}

export type PoolDiff = {
  added: string[];
  removed: string[];
  clubChanges: { id: string; from: string | null; to: string | null }[];
  capsChanged: number;
  otherChanged: number;
  changed: boolean;
};

export function diffPools(previous: Pool | null, next: Pool): PoolDiff {
  const before = new Map((previous?.players ?? []).map((p) => [p.id, p]));
  const after = new Map(next.players.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
  const rest = (p: PoolPlayer) => JSON.stringify({ ...p, clubId: null, caps: 0, goals: 0, provenance: null });
  const diff: PoolDiff = {
    added: [...after.keys()].filter((id) => !before.has(id)),
    removed: [...before.keys()].filter((id) => !after.has(id)),
    clubChanges: [],
    capsChanged: 0,
    otherChanged: 0,
    changed: previous === null || JSON.stringify(previous) !== JSON.stringify(next),
  };
  for (const [id, p] of after) {
    const old = before.get(id);
    if (!old) continue;
    if (old.clubId !== p.clubId) diff.clubChanges.push({ id, from: old.clubId, to: p.clubId });
    if (old.caps !== p.caps || old.goals !== p.goals) diff.capsChanged++;
    if (rest(old) !== rest(p)) diff.otherChanged++;
  }
  return diff;
}

/** A pool that loses more than a tenth of its footballers means a source broke. */
export function guardChange(previous: Pool | null, next: Pool): string | null {
  if (!previous) return null;
  const before = previous.players.length;
  const after = next.players.length;
  return after < before * 0.9
    ? `the pool would shrink from ${before} to ${after} footballers; refusing (a source probably failed)`
    : null;
}

export function missingSeasons(honours: PoolHonour[], competition: Competition, from: number, to: number): number[] {
  const have = new Set(honours.filter((h) => h.competition === competition).map((h) => h.seasonStart));
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let y = from; y <= to; y++) if (!have.has(y)) out.push(y);
  return out;
}

const season = (start: number) => `${start}–${String((start + 1) % 100).padStart(2, "0")}`;
const percent = (count: number, total: number) => (total === 0 ? "0%" : `${Math.round((count * 100) / total)}%`);
const list = (ids: string[]) => (ids.length === 0 ? "none" : ids.slice(0, 50).join(", ") + (ids.length > 50 ? ", …" : ""));

export function renderReport(input: {
  pool: Pool;
  diff: PoolDiff;
  statuses: Record<string, SourceStatus>;
  today: string;
}): string {
  const { pool, diff, statuses, today } = input;
  const clubName = new Map(pool.clubs.map((c) => [c.id, c.nameLatin]));
  const club = (id: string | null) => (id === null ? "none" : (clubName.get(id) ?? id));
  const byQid = new Map(pool.players.map((p) => [p.wikidataId, p.nameLatin]));
  const active = pool.players.filter((p) => p.pools.active).length;
  const legend = pool.players.filter((p) => p.pools.legend).length;
  const both = pool.players.filter((p) => p.pools.active && p.pools.legend).length;
  const out = [`# Nightly pool, ${today}`, "", "| Source | Status | Read on | Note |", "| --- | --- | --- | --- |"];
  for (const [name, s] of Object.entries(statuses)) out.push(`| ${name} | ${s.status} | ${s.retrievedAt ?? "never"} | ${s.note ?? ""} |`);
  out.push(
    "",
    `**${pool.players.length} footballers** (${active} active, ${legend} legends, ${both} in both), ${pool.clubs.length} clubs, ${pool.honours.length} honours.`,
    "",
    `## Club changes, review each (${diff.clubChanges.length})`,
    "",
    ...(diff.clubChanges.length === 0 ? ["None."] : diff.clubChanges.map((c) => `- ${c.id}: ${club(c.from)} → ${club(c.to)}`)),
    "",
    "## Other changes",
    "",
    `- Added (${diff.added.length}): ${list(diff.added)}`,
    `- Removed (${diff.removed.length}): ${list(diff.removed)}`,
    `- Caps or goals changed: ${diff.capsChanged}. Other fields changed: ${diff.otherChanged}.`,
    "",
    "## Coverage",
    "",
    "| Field | Footballers | Share |",
    "| --- | --- | --- |",
    ...computeCoverage(pool.players).map((r) => `| ${r.field} | ${r.count} of ${r.total} | ${percent(r.count, r.total)} |`),
    "",
    `## Flags (${pool.flags.length})`,
  );
  const kinds = new Map<string, typeof pool.flags>();
  for (const flag of pool.flags) kinds.set(flag.kind, [...(kinds.get(flag.kind) ?? []), flag]);
  for (const [kind, flags] of kinds) {
    out.push("", `### ${kind} (${flags.length})`, "");
    for (const f of flags.slice(0, 100)) out.push(`- ${byQid.get(f.subject) ?? f.subject} (${f.subject}): ${f.detail}`);
    if (flags.length > 100) out.push(`- … ${flags.length - 100} more in data/pool.json`);
  }
  const last = Number(today.slice(0, 4)) - 1;
  const gaps = (c: Competition) => missingSeasons(pool.honours, c, 1990, last).map(season).join(", ") || "none";
  out.push(
    "",
    "## Honours to curate (data/curated/honours.json)",
    "",
    `- Ligue 1 seasons without a winner since 1990–91: ${gaps("tn_ligue1")}`,
    `- Tunisian Cup seasons without a winner since 1990–91: ${gaps("tn_cup")}`,
  );
  return out.join("\n") + "\n";
}
```

`src/pipeline/check.ts`:

```ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { validateOverrides } from "./overrides.ts";
import { competitions, regions } from "./types.ts";
import type { CuratedHonour, GovernorateRow } from "./types.ts";
import { validatePool } from "./validate.ts";

/** Every file in data/ that people edit or the job writes, checked in CI. */
export async function checkData(root: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const read = async (name: string): Promise<unknown> => {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, "data", name), "utf8"));
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as { code?: string }).code !== "ENOENT") errors.push(`data/${name}: ${(error as Error).message}`);
      return undefined;
    }
  };

  const governorates = await read("curated/governorates.json");
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  if (!Array.isArray(governorates) || governorates.length !== 24) {
    errors.push("data/curated/governorates.json: must list the 24 governorates");
  } else {
    for (const row of governorates as GovernorateRow[]) {
      if (!/^[a-z]+(?:-[a-z]+)*$/.test(row.id) || ids.has(row.id)) errors.push(`governorate ${row.id}: bad or duplicate id`);
      ids.add(row.id);
      if (!regions.includes(row.region)) errors.push(`governorate ${row.id}: unknown region ${row.region}`);
      if (!row.nameLatin || !row.nameArabic || !row.nameFrench) errors.push(`governorate ${row.id}: a name is missing`);
    }
  }

  const overrides = (await read("overrides.json")) ?? null;
  errors.push(...validateOverrides(overrides, ids).errors.map((e) => `data/overrides.json: ${e}`));

  const ligue1 = (await read("curated/ligue1-clubs.json")) as { season?: unknown; clubs?: unknown } | undefined;
  if (
    ligue1 !== undefined &&
    (typeof ligue1?.season !== "string" ||
      !Array.isArray(ligue1.clubs) ||
      ligue1.clubs.length > 20 ||
      !ligue1.clubs.every((c) => typeof c === "string" && c !== ""))
  ) {
    errors.push("data/curated/ligue1-clubs.json: must be { season, clubs: [up to 20 English titles] }");
  }

  const honours = await read("curated/honours.json");
  if (honours !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(honours)) {
      errors.push("data/curated/honours.json: must be a list");
    } else {
      for (const h of honours as CuratedHonour[]) {
        const ok =
          competitions.includes(h.competition) &&
          Number.isInteger(h.seasonStart) &&
          /^Q\d+$/.test(h.clubWikidataId) &&
          typeof h.by === "string" &&
          h.by !== "" &&
          /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(h.at ?? "");
        if (!ok) errors.push(`data/curated/honours.json: invalid entry ${JSON.stringify(h)}`);
      }
    }
  }

  const pool = await read("pool.json");
  if (pool !== undefined) errors.push(...validatePool(pool, ids).map((e) => `data/pool.json: ${e}`));
  return errors;
}
```

---

### Task 8: The build, with its source cache, and the first real pool

**Files:**
- Create: `src/pipeline/run.ts`, `src/pipeline/run.test.ts`, `data/README.md`, `data/pool.json` and `data/report.md` (written by the first run)
- Modify: `src/pipeline/cli.ts`, `package.json` (script `data:build`), `.gitignore`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything in `src/pipeline/` from Tasks 3–7.
- Produces: `run(deps: RunDeps): Promise<RunResult>` with `RunDeps = { root: string; today: string; wikidata: PoliteClient; wikipedia: PoliteClient; github: PoliteClient; log: (line: string) => void }` and `RunResult = { ok: true; changed: boolean } | { ok: false; reason: string }`; `pnpm data:build` (`node src/pipeline/cli.ts build`), exit code 1 when the run refuses; the first committed `data/pool.json`.

Order of a run: read the previous pool and the curated files → Wikidata (players, aliases, memberships, honours) → English and French infoboxes for male footballers born in 1965 or later → redirects of current-club links → Wikidata clubs (by id and by title) → Commons → martj42 → merge → validate → shrink guard → write `pool.json` and `report.md`. Each source goes through `cached()`: success refreshes `data/cache/<source>.json`; failure falls back to it and says so; Wikidata failing with no cache stops the run.

`src/pipeline/run.test.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { PoliteClient } from "./http.ts";
import { run } from "./run.ts";
import type { Pool } from "./types.ts";

const uri = (id: string) => ({ type: "uri", value: `http://www.wikidata.org/entity/${id}` });
const lit = (value: string) => ({ type: "literal", value });
const result = (...bindings: object[]) => ({ results: { bindings } });

const answers = {
  players: result({
    p: uri("Q1001"),
    enLabel: lit("Test Footballer"),
    arLabel: lit("لاعب تجربة"),
    birth: lit("1995-05-05T00:00:00Z"),
    positions: lit("midfielder"),
    birthPlace: uri("Q3572"),
    birthPlaceName: lit("Tunis"),
    birthCountry: lit("TN"),
    governorates: lit("Tunis Governorate"),
    image: { type: "uri", value: "http://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:FilePath/Test.jpg" },
    enwiki: lit("Test Footballer"),
  }),
  aliases: result({ p: uri("Q1001"), aliases: lit("Testo") }),
  memberships: result({ p: uri("Q1001"), team: uri("Q27971"), start: lit("2015-01-01T00:00:00Z"), isNational: lit("1") }),
  honours: result({ compName: lit("Tunisian Ligue Professionnelle 1"), seasonLabel: lit("2012–13 Tunisian Ligue Professionnelle 1"), winner: uri("Q2001") }),
  clubs: result({ club: uri("Q2001"), en: lit("Club Africain"), iso: lit("TN"), leagues: lit("Q794235"), enTitle: lit("Club Africain") }),
};

const infobox = `{{Infobox football biography
| name = Test Footballer
| position = [[Midfielder]]
| currentclub = [[Club Africain]]
| clubs1 = [[Club Africain]]
| years1 = 2014–
| caps1 = 120
| goals1 = 10
| pcupdate = 1 October 2026
| nationalyears1 = 2015–
| nationalteam1 = [[Tunisia national football team|Tunisia]]
| nationalcaps1 = 30
| nationalgoals1 = 2
| ntupdate = 1 October 2026
}}`;

const csv = "date,home_team,away_team,home_score,away_score,tournament,city,country,neutral\n2026-09-09,Tunisia,Mali,1,0,Friendly,Tunis,Tunisia,FALSE\n";

function wikidata(fail = false): PoliteClient {
  return {
    async getJson(_url, init) {
      if (fail) throw new Error("HTTP 429 from query.wikidata.org");
      const query = new URLSearchParams(String(init?.body)).get("query") ?? "";
      if (query.includes("skos:altLabel")) return answers.aliases;
      if (query.includes("pq:P580")) return answers.memberships;
      if (query.includes("wdt:P1346")) return answers.honours;
      if (query.includes("VALUES ?club")) return answers.clubs;
      return answers.players;
    },
    getText: async () => "",
  };
}

function wikipedia(fail = false): PoliteClient {
  return {
    async getJson(url) {
      if (fail) throw new Error("HTTP 429 from en.wikipedia.org");
      if (url.includes("commons.wikimedia.org")) {
        return {
          query: {
            pages: [{
              title: "File:Test.jpg",
              imageinfo: [{ thumburl: "https://upload.wikimedia.org/t.jpg", width: 800, height: 600, descriptionurl: "https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Test.jpg", extmetadata: { LicenseShortName: { value: "CC BY-SA 4.0" } } }],
            }],
          },
        };
      }
      if (url.includes("prop=revisions")) {
        return { query: { pages: [{ title: "Test Footballer", revisions: [{ revid: 1, slots: { main: { content: infobox } } }] }] } };
      }
      return { query: {} };
    },
    getText: async () => "",
  };
}

const github: PoliteClient = { getJson: async () => ({}), getText: async () => csv };

async function setup(): Promise<string> {
  const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), "kora-run-"));
  await mkdir(path.join(root, "data", "curated"), { recursive: true });
  const write = (name: string, value: unknown) => writeFile(path.join(root, "data", name), JSON.stringify(value));
  await write("curated/governorates.json", JSON.parse(await readFile("data/curated/governorates.json", "utf8")));
  await write("curated/ligue1-clubs.json", { season: "2026–27", clubs: ["Club Africain"] });
  await write("curated/honours.json", []);
  await write("overrides.json", { players: {}, clubTitles: {} });
  return root;
}

const deps = (root: string, over: Partial<Parameters<typeof run>[0]> = {}) => ({
  root,
  today: "2026-10-04",
  wikidata: wikidata(),
  wikipedia: wikipedia(),
  github,
  log: () => {},
  ...over,
});

const readPool = async (root: string) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, "data", "pool.json"), "utf8")) as Pool;

describe("run", () => {
  it("builds and writes the pool and the report from the sources", async () => {
    const root = await setup();
    expect(await run(deps(root))).toEqual({ ok: true, changed: true });

    const pool = await readPool(root);
    expect(pool.players).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(pool.players[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "test-footballer",
      clubId: "club-africain",
      governorate: "tunis",
      caps: 30,
      aliases: ["Testo"],
      pools: { active: true, legend: true },
      photo: { licence: "CC BY-SA 4.0" },
    });
    expect(pool.clubs.map((c) => [c.id, c.ligue1])).toEqual([["club-africain", true]]);
    expect(pool.honours).toEqual([{ competition: "tn_ligue1", seasonStart: 2012, clubId: "club-africain", source: "wikidata" }]);
    expect(await readFile(path.join(root, "data", "report.md"), "utf8")).toContain("# Nightly pool, 2026-10-04");
  });

  it("falls back to the last good answer when a source fails, and says so", async () => {
    const root = await setup();
    await run(deps(root));
    const before = await readPool(root);

    expect(await run(deps(root, { today: "2026-10-05", wikipedia: wikipedia(true) }))).toEqual({ ok: true, changed: false });
    expect(await readPool(root)).toEqual(before);
    expect(await readFile(path.join(root, "data", "report.md"), "utf8")).toContain("| infobox-en | cached | 2026-10-04 | HTTP 429 from en.wikipedia.org |");
  });

  it("stops when Wikidata fails and nothing is cached", async () => {
    const root = await setup();
    const outcome = await run(deps(root, { wikidata: wikidata(true) }));
    expect(outcome).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "Wikidata failed and there is no cached copy" });
  });

  it("refuses a pool that shrinks by more than a tenth, leaving the old one", async () => {
    const root = await setup();
    const previous = {
      version: 1,
      players: Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => ({ id: `p${i}`, wikidataId: `Q${9000 + i}`, provenance: {} })),
      clubs: [],
      honours: [],
      flags: [],
    };
    await writeFile(path.join(root, "data", "pool.json"), JSON.stringify(previous));

    const outcome = await run(deps(root));
    expect(outcome.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, "data", "pool.json"), "utf8"))).toEqual(previous);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The run**

`src/pipeline/run.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { commonsUrl, parseCommons } from "./commons.ts";
import type { PoliteClient } from "./http.ts";
import { buildClubIndex } from "./merge.ts";
import { validateOverrides } from "./overrides.ts";
import { buildPool } from "./pool.ts";
import { diffPools, guardChange, renderReport } from "./report.ts";
import { RESULTS_URL, tunisiaMatches } from "./results.ts";
import type {
  CuratedHonour,
  GovernorateRow,
  Infobox,
  Match,
  Photo,
  Pool,
  SourceStatus,
  WdClub,
  WdHonour,
  WdMembership,
  WdPlayer,
} from "./types.ts";
import { validatePool } from "./validate.ts";
import { chunk, parseRevisions, redirectsUrl, revisionsUrl } from "./wiki/fetch.ts";
import { parseEnInfobox } from "./wiki/infobox-en.ts";
import { parseFrInfobox } from "./wiki/infobox-fr.ts";
import { parseAliases, parseClubs, parseHonours, parseMemberships, parsePlayers } from "./wikidata/parse.ts";
import {
  ALIASES_QUERY,
  clubsQuery,
  HONOURS_QUERY,
  MEMBERSHIPS_QUERY,
  PLAYERS_QUERY,
  sparqlRequest,
} from "./wikidata/queries.ts";

export type RunDeps = {
  root: string;
  today: string;
  wikidata: PoliteClient;
  wikipedia: PoliteClient;
  github: PoliteClient;
  log: (line: string) => void;
};

export type RunResult = { ok: true; changed: boolean } | { ok: false; reason: string };

type Wikidata = {
  players: WdPlayer[];
  aliases: [string, string[]][];
  memberships: WdMembership[];
  honours: WdHonour[];
};

async function readJson<T>(file: string): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8")) as T;
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "ENOENT") return null;
    throw error;
  }
}

function groupBy<T>(items: T[], key: (item: T) => string): Map<string, T[]> {
  const groups = new Map<string, T[]>();
  for (const item of items) groups.set(key(item), [...(groups.get(key(item)) ?? []), item]);
  return groups;
}

const present = <T>(value: T | null): value is T => value !== null;

export async function run(deps: RunDeps): Promise<RunResult> {
  const data = path.join(deps.root, "data");
  const statuses: Record<string, SourceStatus> = {};

  /** Fetch, or fall back to the last good answer, recording which. */
  async function cached<T>(name: string, fetcher: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | null> {
    const file = path.join(data, "cache", `${name}.json`);
    try {
      const value = await fetcher();
      await mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(file, JSON.stringify({ retrievedAt: deps.today, value }));
      statuses[name] = { status: "fresh", retrievedAt: deps.today };
      return value;
    } catch (error) {
      const note = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      const last = await readJson<{ retrievedAt: string; value: T }>(file);
      deps.log(`${name}: ${note}; ${last ? `using the copy from ${last.retrievedAt}` : "no cached copy"}`);
      statuses[name] = last
        ? { status: "cached", retrievedAt: last.retrievedAt, note }
        : { status: "failed", retrievedAt: null, note };
      return last?.value ?? null;
    }
  }

  const previous = await readJson<Pool>(path.join(data, "pool.json"));
  const governorates = (await readJson<GovernorateRow[]>(path.join(data, "curated", "governorates.json"))) ?? [];
  const governorateIds = new Set(governorates.map((g) => g.id));
  const checked = validateOverrides(await readJson(path.join(data, "overrides.json")), governorateIds);
  if (checked.errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, reason: `overrides.json: ${checked.errors.join("; ")}` };
  const overrides = checked.overrides;
  const ligue1 = (await readJson<{ clubs: string[] }>(path.join(data, "curated", "ligue1-clubs.json"))) ?? { clubs: [] };
  const curatedHonours = (await readJson<CuratedHonour[]>(path.join(data, "curated", "honours.json"))) ?? [];

  const sparql = async (query: string) => {
    const { url, init } = sparqlRequest(query);
    return deps.wikidata.getJson(url, init);
  };

  const wikidata = await cached<Wikidata>("wikidata", async () => ({
    players: parsePlayers(await sparql(PLAYERS_QUERY)),
    aliases: parseAliases(await sparql(ALIASES_QUERY)),
    memberships: parseMemberships(await sparql(MEMBERSHIPS_QUERY)),
    honours: parseHonours(await sparql(HONOURS_QUERY)),
  }));
  if (!wikidata) return { ok: false, reason: "Wikidata failed and there is no cached copy" };
  const aliases = new Map(wikidata.aliases);
  const players = wikidata.players.map((p) => ({ ...p, aliases: aliases.get(p.qid) ?? [] }));
  const wanted = players.filter((p) => p.male && p.birthDate !== null && p.birthDate >= "1965");

  const infoboxes = async (lang: "en" | "fr") =>
    (await cached<Infobox[]>(`infobox-${lang}`, async () => {
      const titles = wanted.map((p) => p.titles[lang]).filter(present);
      const boxes: Infobox[] = [];
      for (const batch of chunk(titles)) {
        const { pages } = parseRevisions(await deps.wikipedia.getJson(revisionsUrl(lang, batch)));
        for (const page of pages) {
          const box = lang === "en" ? parseEnInfobox(page.title, page.wikitext) : parseFrInfobox(page.title, page.wikitext);
          if (box) boxes.push(box);
        }
      }
      return boxes;
    })) ?? [];
  const en = await infoboxes("en");
  const fr = await infoboxes("fr");

  // Current-club links often go through a redirect; resolve them before
  // asking Wikidata which club each title is.
  const redirects =
    (await cached<{ en: [string, string][]; fr: [string, string][] }>("redirects", async () => {
      const out: { en: [string, string][]; fr: [string, string][] } = { en: [], fr: [] };
      for (const [lang, boxes] of [["en", en], ["fr", fr]] as const) {
        const titles = [...new Set(boxes.map((b) => b.currentClub).filter(present))];
        for (const batch of chunk(titles)) {
          out[lang].push(...parseRevisions(await deps.wikipedia.getJson(redirectsUrl(lang, batch))).aliases);
        }
      }
      return out;
    })) ?? { en: [], fr: [] };

  const overrideClubs = Object.values(overrides.players).flatMap((o) => (o.club?.value ? [o.club.value] : []));
  const clubQids = new Set([
    ...wikidata.memberships.filter((m) => !m.national).map((m) => m.teamQid),
    ...wikidata.honours.map((h) => h.winnerQid),
    ...curatedHonours.map((h) => h.clubWikidataId),
    ...Object.values(overrides.clubTitles),
    ...overrideClubs,
  ]);
  const titlesOf = (boxes: Infobox[], moved: [string, string][]) =>
    new Set([...boxes.flatMap((b) => [b.currentClub, ...b.spells.map((s) => s.clubTitle)]).filter(present), ...moved.map(([, to]) => to)]);
  const enTitles = titlesOf(en, redirects.en);
  for (const title of ligue1.clubs) enTitles.add(title);
  const frTitles = titlesOf(fr, redirects.fr);
  const clubs = await cached<WdClub[]>("clubs", async () =>
    parseClubs(await sparql(clubsQuery([...clubQids], [...enTitles], [...frTitles]))),
  );
  if (!clubs) return { ok: false, reason: "Wikidata clubs failed and there is no cached copy" };

  const photos =
    (await cached<Photo[]>("commons", async () => {
      const files = wanted.map((p) => p.imageFile).filter(present);
      const out: Photo[] = [];
      for (const batch of chunk(files)) out.push(...parseCommons(await deps.wikipedia.getJson(commonsUrl(batch))).values());
      return out;
    })) ?? [];
  const matches =
    (await cached<Match[]>("martj42", async () => tunisiaMatches(await deps.github.getText(RESULTS_URL)))) ?? [];

  const pool = buildPool({
    today: deps.today,
    players,
    memberships: groupBy(wikidata.memberships, (m) => m.playerQid),
    index: buildClubIndex(clubs, { en: new Map(redirects.en), fr: new Map(redirects.fr) }, overrides.clubTitles),
    infoboxes: { en: new Map(en.map((b) => [b.title, b])), fr: new Map(fr.map((b) => [b.title, b])) },
    photos: new Map(photos.map((p) => [p.file, p])),
    tunisiaMatches: matches,
    overrides,
    governorateIds,
    honours: wikidata.honours,
    curatedHonours,
    ligue1Titles: ligue1.clubs,
    previous,
  });

  const invalid = validatePool(pool, governorateIds);
  if (invalid.length > 0) return { ok: false, reason: `the new pool is invalid: ${invalid.slice(0, 5).join("; ")}` };
  const refusal = guardChange(previous, pool);
  if (refusal) return { ok: false, reason: refusal };

  const diff = diffPools(previous, pool);
  await writeFile(path.join(data, "pool.json"), JSON.stringify(pool, null, 2) + "\n");
  await writeFile(path.join(data, "report.md"), renderReport({ pool, diff, statuses, today: deps.today }));
  return { ok: true, changed: diff.changed };
}
```

Run: `pnpm test src/pipeline/run.test.ts`
Expected: PASS (4 tests).

- [ ] **Step 3: The `build` command**

Replace `src/pipeline/cli.ts`:

```ts
import { checkData } from "./check.ts";
import { createPoliteClient } from "./http.ts";
import { run } from "./run.ts";

// Run with Node directly:
//   node src/pipeline/cli.ts build   fetch, merge, write data/pool.json and data/report.md
//   node src/pipeline/cli.ts check   check every file in data/
const command = process.argv[2];

if (command === "build") {
  const result = await run({
    root: process.cwd(),
    today: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    wikidata: createPoliteClient({ minGapMs: 2_000, maxRetries: 3 }),
    // Wikipedia and Commons share one client: Wikimedia's limits are per client.
    wikipedia: createPoliteClient({ minGapMs: 5_000, maxRetries: 3 }),
    github: createPoliteClient({ minGapMs: 0, maxRetries: 2 }),
    log: (line) => console.log(line),
  });
  if (!result.ok) {
    console.error(`data:build refused: ${result.reason}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.log(result.changed ? "data:build: the pool changed" : "data:build: no change");
} else if (command === "check") {
  const errors = await checkData(process.cwd());
  for (const error of errors) console.error(error);
  if (errors.length > 0) process.exit(1);
  console.log("data:check: ok");
} else {
  console.error("usage: node src/pipeline/cli.ts build|check");
  process.exit(2);
}
```

In `package.json` scripts add `"data:build": "node src/pipeline/cli.ts build"`. In `.gitignore`, under `# test output`, add:

```
# last good answers from the data sources (kept by actions/cache in CI)
/data/cache/
```

`data/README.md`:

```markdown
# Data

| File                         | Written by                        | What it is                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `pool.json`                  | the nightly job (`pnpm data:build`) | Footballers, clubs and honours the games use; every field names its source                 |
| `report.md`                  | the nightly job                   | What changed, coverage, and flags to review; also the nightly pull request's description |
| `overrides.json`             | Jalel                             | Corrections that always win, each with who decided and when                                 |
| `curated/governorates.json`  | Jalel                             | The 24 governorates, their names and their region                                           |
| `curated/ligue1-clubs.json`  | Jalel                             | This season's Ligue 1 clubs, as English Wikipedia titles                                    |
| `curated/honours.json`       | Jalel                             | Club titles Wikidata does not have                                                          |
| `cache/` (not committed)     | the job                           | The last good answer from each source, used when a source fails                             |

Nothing here reaches the database until a pull request is merged: the deploy that follows writes `pool.json` to that environment's database.

## An override

Keyed by the footballer's Wikidata id. Every value says who decided and when:

    {
      "players": {
        "Q2836275": {
          "club": { "value": "Q201", "by": "jalel", "at": "2026-10-05", "note": "Al Ahly since 2016" },
          "position": { "value": "defender", "by": "jalel", "at": "2026-10-05" }
        }
      },
      "clubTitles": { "en:Esperance de Tunis": "Q202" }
    }

Fields: `club` (a club's Wikidata id, or `null` for none), `position`, `positionDetail`, `caps`, `goals`, `capsAsOf`, `governorate`, `birthCountry`, `nameArabic`, `aliases`, `pools` (`{ "active": true, "legend": false }`), `exclude`. `clubTitles` pins a Wikipedia link title to a club. Edit overrides on `main`, never on the `data/nightly` branch, which is rebuilt every night.

## Sources and licences

- **Wikidata**: identity, names, birth, position, clubs. CC0.
- **English and French Wikipedia**: current club, career and caps, read from infoboxes. Wikipedia's text is CC BY-SA 4.0; the pool records which article each value came from, so the site can credit it.
- **Wikimedia Commons**: photos. Each keeps its author, licence, licence link and file page, shown wherever the photo appears.
- **martj42/international_results**: Tunisia's results, to tell when caps are out of date. CC0.
```

Append to `README.md`:

```markdown

## Data

The footballers come from Wikidata, English and French Wikipedia, Wikimedia Commons and a CC0 results dataset, rebuilt every night and reviewed as a pull request. Sources, licences and how corrections work: [data/README.md](data/README.md).
```

### Task 9: Into Postgres on deploy, and the nightly pull request

**Files:**
- Create: `src/pipeline/sync.ts`, `src/pipeline/sync.db.test.ts`, `src/pipeline/sync-cli.ts`, `.github/workflows/nightly-data.yml`, `scripts/open-data-pr.sh`
- Modify: `package.json` (`vercel-build`, script `data:sync`), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (`workflow_dispatch`), `docs/schema.md` (one sentence)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Pool`, `GovernorateRow` (`types.ts`); `validatePool` (`validate.ts`); the tables from Task 2; `createTestClient`, `resetDatabase`, `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (`src/db/testing.ts`).
- Produces: `Queryable = { query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<{ rowCount: number | null }> }`, `SyncCounts = { governorates: number; clubs: number; players: number; spells: number; honours: number; leftPool: number }`, `syncPool(db: Queryable, pool: Pool, governorates: GovernorateRow[]): Promise<SyncCounts>`; `vercel-build` = `prisma migrate deploy && node src/pipeline/sync-cli.ts && next build`; the `Nightly data` workflow.

The sync uses `pg` with SQL, not the Prisma client: Node runs `sync-cli.ts` by type stripping, and the generated Prisma client imports files without extensions, which Node will not resolve. The SQL is proven against Postgres by the test below. Footballers are never deleted: one who leaves the pool keeps his row with both pool flags off.

```ts
import pg from "pg";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createTestClient, resetDatabase, TEST_DATABASE_URL } from "@/db/testing";
import { syncPool } from "./sync.ts";
import type { GovernorateRow, Pool, PoolPlayer } from "./types.ts";

const db = createTestClient(); // checks that the URL is local, first
const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: TEST_DATABASE_URL });
const sql = { query: (text: string, values?: unknown[]) => client.query(text, values) };

beforeAll(async () => {
  await client.connect();
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await client.end();
  await db.$disconnect();
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetDatabase(db);
});

const governorates: GovernorateRow[] = [
  { id: "sfax", nameLatin: "Sfax", nameArabic: "صفاقس", nameFrench: "Sfax", region: "centre_east" },
];

const maaloul: PoolPlayer = {
  id: "ali-maaloul", wikidataId: "Q2836275", nameLatin: "Ali Maâloul", nameArabic: "علي معلول", nameFrench: "Ali Maâloul",
  aliases: ["maaloul", "ma3loul"], position: "defender", positionDetail: "Left-back", birthDate: "1990-01-01",
  birthPlace: "Sfax", birthCountry: "TN", governorate: "sfax", clubId: "al-ahly", caps: 80, goals: 1, capsAsOf: "2026-09-19",
  history: [
    { clubId: "cs-sfaxien", clubName: "CS Sfaxien", from: 2010, to: 2016, apps: 120, goals: 5, loan: false },
    { clubId: "al-ahly", clubName: "Al Ahly SC", from: 2016, to: null, apps: null, goals: null, loan: false },
  ],
  photo: { file: "File:Ali Maaloul.jpg", thumbUrl: "https://upload.wikimedia.org/a.jpg", width: 800, height: 1000, licence: "CC BY-SA 4.0", licenceUrl: "https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0", author: "Someone", sourceUrl: "https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Ali_Maaloul.jpg", attributionRequired: true },
  wiki: { en: "Ali Maâloul", fr: "Ali Maâloul", ar: null }, pools: { active: true, legend: true },
  provenance: { clubId: { source: "enwiki", retrievedAt: "2026-10-04", asOf: "2026-09-19", ref: "Ali Maâloul" } },
};

const msakni: PoolPlayer = {
  ...maaloul, id: "youssef-msakni", wikidataId: "Q2409513", nameLatin: "Youssef Msakni", aliases: [], governorate: null,
  birthCountry: null, clubId: null, history: [{ clubId: "cs-sfaxien", clubName: "CS Sfaxien", from: 2008, to: 2009, apps: 1, goals: 0, loan: false }],
  photo: null, pools: { active: false, legend: true }, provenance: {},
};

function pool(players: PoolPlayer[], country = "TN"): Pool {
  return {
    version: 1,
    players,
    clubs: [
      { id: "al-ahly", wikidataId: "Q201", nameLatin: "Al Ahly SC", nameArabic: null, nameFrench: null, country: "EG", confederation: "CAF", leagueWikidataId: null, ligue1: false },
      { id: "cs-sfaxien", wikidataId: "Q202", nameLatin: "CS Sfaxien", nameArabic: "النادي الرياضي الصفاقسي", nameFrench: null, country, confederation: "CAF", leagueWikidataId: "Q794235", ligue1: true },
    ],
    honours: [{ competition: "tn_ligue1", seasonStart: 2012, clubId: "cs-sfaxien", source: "wikidata" }],
    flags: [],
  };
}

describe("syncPool", () => {
  it("writes governorates, clubs, footballers, careers and honours", async () => {
    expect(await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul, msakni]), governorates)).toEqual({
      governorates: 1, clubs: 2, players: 2, spells: 3, honours: 1, leftPool: 0,
    });

    const row = await db.player.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: "ali-maaloul" },
      include: { career: { orderBy: { seq: "asc" } }, club: true, governorate: true },
    });
    expect(row.aliases).toEqual(["maaloul", "ma3loul"]);
    expect(row.club?.country).toBe("EG");
    expect(row.governorate?.region).toBe("centre_east");
    expect(row.capsAsOf?.toISOString().slice(0, 10)).toBe("2026-09-19");
    expect(row.photoLicence).toBe("CC BY-SA 4.0");
    expect(row.photoSourceUrl).toBe("https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Ali_Maaloul.jpg");
    expect(row.career.map((s) => [s.seq, s.clubName, s.toYear])).toEqual([
      [0, "CS Sfaxien", 2016],
      [1, "Al Ahly SC", null],
    ]);
    expect(row.provenance).toEqual(maaloul.provenance);
  });

  it("gives the same database when run twice", async () => {
    await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul, msakni]), governorates);
    await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul, msakni]), governorates);
    expect([await db.player.count(), await db.playerClub.count(), await db.honour.count(), await db.club.count()]).toEqual([2, 3, 1, 2]);
  });

  it("keeps a footballer who left the pool, out of both pools", async () => {
    await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul, msakni]), governorates);
    const counts = await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul]), governorates);

    expect(counts.leftPool).toBe(1);
    const left = await db.player.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: "youssef-msakni" } });
    expect([left.poolActive, left.poolLegend]).toEqual([false, false]);
  });

  it("replaces a career rather than adding to it", async () => {
    await syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul]), governorates);
    await syncPool(sql, pool([{ ...maaloul, history: [maaloul.history[1]] }]), governorates);
    expect(await db.playerClub.count({ where: { playerId: "ali-maaloul" } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it("writes nothing when one row breaks a rule", async () => {
    await expect(syncPool(sql, pool([maaloul], "tn"), governorates)).rejects.toThrow(/clubs_country_iso/);
    expect([await db.governorate.count(), await db.club.count(), await db.player.count()]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The sync**

`src/pipeline/sync.ts`:

```ts
import type { GovernorateRow, Pool } from "./types.ts";

// data/pool.json → Postgres, in one transaction: all or nothing. Rows are
// passed as one JSON parameter per table and unpacked by jsonb_to_recordset.

export type Queryable = {
  query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<{ rowCount: number | null }>;
};

export type SyncCounts = {
  governorates: number;
  clubs: number;
  players: number;
  spells: number;
  honours: number;
  leftPool: number;
};

const GOVERNORATES = `
INSERT INTO governorates (id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, region)
SELECT id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, region::"Region"
FROM jsonb_to_recordset($1::jsonb)
  AS r(id text, name_latin text, name_arabic text, name_french text, region text)
ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
  name_latin = EXCLUDED.name_latin, name_arabic = EXCLUDED.name_arabic,
  name_french = EXCLUDED.name_french, region = EXCLUDED.region`;

const CLUBS = `
INSERT INTO clubs (id, wikidata_id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, country,
  confederation, league_wikidata_id, ligue1, updated_at)
SELECT id, wikidata_id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, country,
  confederation::"Confederation", league_wikidata_id, ligue1, now()
FROM jsonb_to_recordset($1::jsonb)
  AS r(id text, wikidata_id text, name_latin text, name_arabic text, name_french text,
       country text, confederation text, league_wikidata_id text, ligue1 boolean)
ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
  wikidata_id = EXCLUDED.wikidata_id, name_latin = EXCLUDED.name_latin,
  name_arabic = EXCLUDED.name_arabic, name_french = EXCLUDED.name_french,
  country = EXCLUDED.country, confederation = EXCLUDED.confederation,
  league_wikidata_id = EXCLUDED.league_wikidata_id, ligue1 = EXCLUDED.ligue1,
  updated_at = now()`;

const CLUBS_LEFT_LIGUE1 = `
UPDATE clubs SET ligue1 = false, updated_at = now()
WHERE ligue1 AND NOT (id = ANY($1::text[]))`;

const PLAYER_COLUMNS = `id, wikidata_id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, aliases,
  position, position_detail, birth_date, birth_place, birth_country, governorate,
  caps, goals, caps_as_of, club_id, photo_file, photo_url, photo_author,
  photo_licence, photo_licence_url, photo_source_url, wiki_en, wiki_fr, wiki_ar,
  pool_active, pool_legend, provenance`;

const PLAYERS = `
INSERT INTO players (${PLAYER_COLUMNS}, updated_at)
SELECT id, wikidata_id, name_latin, name_arabic, name_french, aliases,
  position::"Position", position_detail, birth_date, birth_place, birth_country, governorate,
  caps, goals, caps_as_of, club_id, photo_file, photo_url, photo_author,
  photo_licence, photo_licence_url, photo_source_url, wiki_en, wiki_fr, wiki_ar,
  pool_active, pool_legend, provenance, now()
FROM jsonb_to_recordset($1::jsonb)
  AS r(id text, wikidata_id text, name_latin text, name_arabic text, name_french text,
       aliases text[], position text, position_detail text, birth_date date,
       birth_place text, birth_country text, governorate text, caps int, goals int,
       caps_as_of date, club_id text, photo_file text, photo_url text, photo_author text,
       photo_licence text, photo_licence_url text, photo_source_url text, wiki_en text,
       wiki_fr text, wiki_ar text, pool_active boolean, pool_legend boolean, provenance jsonb)
ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
  ${PLAYER_COLUMNS.split(",")
    .map((c) => c.trim())
    .filter((c) => c !== "id")
    .map((c) => `${c} = EXCLUDED.${c}`)
    .join(", ")},
  updated_at = now()`;

const PLAYERS_LEFT_POOL = `
UPDATE players SET pool_active = false, pool_legend = false, updated_at = now()
WHERE (pool_active OR pool_legend) AND NOT (id = ANY($1::text[]))`;

const SPELLS_CLEAR = `DELETE FROM player_clubs WHERE player_id = ANY($1::text[])`;

const SPELLS = `
INSERT INTO player_clubs (player_id, seq, club_id, club_name, from_year, to_year, apps, goals, loan)
SELECT player_id, seq, club_id, club_name, from_year, to_year, apps, goals, loan
FROM jsonb_to_recordset($1::jsonb)
  AS r(player_id text, seq int, club_id text, club_name text, from_year int,
       to_year int, apps int, goals int, loan boolean)`;

const HONOURS = `
INSERT INTO honours (id, competition, season_start, club_id, source)
SELECT gen_random_uuid(), competition::"Competition", season_start, club_id, source
FROM jsonb_to_recordset($1::jsonb)
  AS r(competition text, season_start int, club_id text, source text)
ON CONFLICT (competition, season_start) DO UPDATE SET
  club_id = EXCLUDED.club_id, source = EXCLUDED.source`;

export async function syncPool(db: Queryable, pool: Pool, governorates: GovernorateRow[]): Promise<SyncCounts> {
  const json = (rows: object[]) => [JSON.stringify(rows)];
  const ids = pool.players.map((p) => p.id);
  await db.query("BEGIN");
  try {
    const counts: SyncCounts = {
      governorates: (
        await db.query(GOVERNORATES, json(governorates.map((g) => ({ id: g.id, name_latin: g.nameLatin, name_arabic: g.nameArabic, name_french: g.nameFrench, region: g.region }))))
      ).rowCount ?? 0,
      clubs: (
        await db.query(CLUBS, json(pool.clubs.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, wikidata_id: c.wikidataId, name_latin: c.nameLatin, name_arabic: c.nameArabic, name_french: c.nameFrench, country: c.country, confederation: c.confederation, league_wikidata_id: c.leagueWikidataId, ligue1: c.ligue1 }))))
      ).rowCount ?? 0,
      players: 0,
      spells: 0,
      honours: 0,
      leftPool: 0,
    };
    await db.query(CLUBS_LEFT_LIGUE1, [pool.clubs.map((c) => c.id)]);
    counts.players =
      (
        await db.query(PLAYERS, json(pool.players.map((p) => ({
          id: p.id, wikidata_id: p.wikidataId, name_latin: p.nameLatin, name_arabic: p.nameArabic, name_french: p.nameFrench,
          aliases: p.aliases, position: p.position, position_detail: p.positionDetail, birth_date: p.birthDate,
          birth_place: p.birthPlace, birth_country: p.birthCountry, governorate: p.governorate, caps: p.caps, goals: p.goals,
          caps_as_of: p.capsAsOf, club_id: p.clubId, photo_file: p.photo?.file ?? null, photo_url: p.photo?.thumbUrl ?? null,
          photo_author: p.photo?.author ?? null, photo_licence: p.photo?.licence ?? null, photo_licence_url: p.photo?.licenceUrl ?? null,
          photo_source_url: p.photo?.sourceUrl ?? null, wiki_en: p.wiki.en, wiki_fr: p.wiki.fr, wiki_ar: p.wiki.ar,
          pool_active: p.pools.active, pool_legend: p.pools.legend, provenance: p.provenance,
        }))))
      ).rowCount ?? 0;
    counts.leftPool = (await db.query(PLAYERS_LEFT_POOL, [ids])).rowCount ?? 0;
    await db.query(SPELLS_CLEAR, [ids]);
    counts.spells =
      (
        await db.query(SPELLS, json(pool.players.flatMap((p) => p.history.map((s, seq) => ({
          player_id: p.id, seq, club_id: s.clubId, club_name: s.clubName, from_year: s.from, to_year: s.to, apps: s.apps, goals: s.goals, loan: s.loan,
        })))))
      ).rowCount ?? 0;
    counts.honours =
      (
        await db.query(HONOURS, json(pool.honours.map((h) => ({ competition: h.competition, season_start: h.seasonStart, club_id: h.clubId, source: h.source }))))
      ).rowCount ?? 0;
    await db.query("COMMIT");
    return counts;
  } catch (error) {
    await db.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw error;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The sync at deploy**

`src/pipeline/sync-cli.ts`:

```ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import pg from "pg";
import { syncPool } from "./sync.ts";
import type { GovernorateRow, Pool } from "./types.ts";
import { validatePool } from "./validate.ts";

// Runs inside `vercel-build`, after the migrations and before `next build`,
// against the deployment's own database. Never prints the connection string.
const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED ?? process.env.DATABASE_URL;
if (!url) {
  console.error("sync: DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED or DATABASE_URL must be set");
  process.exit(1);
}

const pool = JSON.parse(await readFile("data/pool.json", "utf8")) as Pool;
const governorates = JSON.parse(await readFile("data/curated/governorates.json", "utf8")) as GovernorateRow[];
const errors = validatePool(pool, new Set(governorates.map((g) => g.id)));
if (errors.length > 0) {
  for (const error of errors.slice(0, 20)) console.error(`sync: ${error}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: url });
await client.connect();
try {
  const counts = await syncPool({ query: (text, values) => client.query(text, values) }, pool, governorates);
  console.log(`sync: ${JSON.stringify(counts)}`);
} finally {
  await client.end();
}
```

In `package.json`: change `vercel-build` to `"prisma migrate deploy && node src/pipeline/sync-cli.ts && next build"` and add `"data:sync": "node src/pipeline/sync-cli.ts"`.

Try it on the local database (the only database this machine may touch):

```bash
DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED=postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5434/kora_test pnpm data:sync
```

In `docs/schema.md`, after the sentence "The pool tables are written only by the data sync …", add: "A footballer's id never changes once given (`assignIds`), so the sync can upsert by id; an id change for the same Wikidata id would break the unique index and fail the deploy rather than duplicate him."

- [ ] **Step 4: The nightly workflow**

`scripts/open-data-pr.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Opens or updates the nightly data pull request. It never merges.
set -euo pipefail

if git diff --quiet -- data/pool.json; then
  echo "pool unchanged: no pull request"
  exit 0
fi

branch=data/nightly
title="data: nightly pool $(date -u +%F)"
git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
git switch -C "$branch"
git add data/pool.json data/report.md
git commit -m "$title"
git push --force origin "$branch"

body=$(mktemp)
head -c 60000 data/report.md > "$body"
printf '\n---\nOpened by the nightly job; never merged automatically. Check each club change. Corrections go in data/overrides.json on main.\n' >> "$body"

if [ "$(gh pr list --head "$branch" --state open --json number --jq length)" = "0" ]; then
  gh pr create --base main --head "$branch" --title "$title" --body-file "$body"
else
  gh pr edit "$branch" --title "$title" --body-file "$body"
fi

# A pull request opened with the workflow's token starts no other workflow.
# A dispatch does, and its checks appear on the pull request's commit.
gh workflow run ci.yml --ref "$branch"
```

`.github/workflows/nightly-data.yml`:

```yaml
name: Nightly data

on:
  schedule:
    # 01:17 UTC, 02:17 in Tunis: off the hour, when Wikimedia is quiet.
    - cron: "17 1 * * *"
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: nightly-data
  cancel-in-progress: false

permissions:
  contents: write
  pull-requests: write
  actions: write

jobs:
  build:
    if: github.repository == 'JalelDridi/kora'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: pnpm
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: Restore the last good answers
        uses: actions/cache/restore@v4
        with:
          path: data/cache
          key: data-cache-${{ github.run_id }}
          restore-keys: data-cache-
      - name: Build the pool
        run: pnpm data:build
      - name: Keep these answers as the last good ones
        uses: actions/cache/save@v4
        with:
          path: data/cache
          key: data-cache-${{ github.run_id }}
      - name: Open or update the pull request
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: bash scripts/open-data-pr.sh
```

In `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, change the trigger to:

```yaml
on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:
```

Check the script parses: `bash -n scripts/open-data-pr.sh` (expected: no output). The workflow itself is first run in Task 11, after the merge, with Jalel's go-ahead.

- [ ] **Step 2: The access rule**

`src/admin/access.ts`:

```ts
// Who may open /admin/*: anyone who sends ADMIN_PASSWORD with HTTP Basic auth
// (decision P16). Without a password of 16 characters or more set, the pages
// do not exist. The pool is public in the repository anyway; this keeps an
// unfinished tool away from visitors and search engines.

export type AdminAccess = "allow" | "challenge" | "not-found";

const MIN_LENGTH = 16;

/** Takes the same time whatever the first difference is. */
export function constantTimeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const x = new TextEncoder().encode(a);
  const y = new TextEncoder().encode(b);
  let diff = x.length ^ y.length;
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(x.length, y.length); i++) diff |= (x[i] ?? 0) ^ (y[i] ?? 0);
  return diff === 0;
}

export function adminAccess(authorization: string | null, password: string | undefined): AdminAccess {
  if (!password || password.length < MIN_LENGTH) return "not-found";
  const match = /^Basic\s+(\S+)$/i.exec(authorization?.trim() ?? "");
  if (!match) return "challenge";
  let decoded: string;
  try {
    decoded = atob(match[1]);
  } catch {
    return "challenge";
  }
  const given = decoded.slice(decoded.indexOf(":") + 1);
  return constantTimeEqual(given, password) ? "allow" : "challenge";
}
```

Expected: PASS (4 tests).

- [ ] **Step 3: Gate `/admin` in the proxy**

Replace `src/proxy.ts`:

```ts
import createMiddleware from "next-intl/middleware";
import { type NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { adminAccess } from "./admin/access";
import { routing } from "./i18n/routing";

// Redirects / to the default locale and maps /ar, /tn and /fr to their
// locale ids. /admin is English, outside the locales, and behind a password.
const intl = createMiddleware(routing);

const NOINDEX = { "X-Robots-Tag": "noindex, nofollow" };

export default function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  if (pathname !== "/admin" && !pathname.startsWith("/admin/") && !pathname.startsWith("/admin.")) {
    return intl(request);
  }
  const access = adminAccess(request.headers.get("authorization"), process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  if (access === "not-found") return new NextResponse("Not found", { status: 404, headers: NOINDEX });
  if (access === "challenge") {
    return new NextResponse("Password required", {
      status: 401,
      headers: { ...NOINDEX, "WWW-Authenticate": 'Basic realm="Kora admin", charset="UTF-8"' },
    });
  }
  const response = NextResponse.next();
  response.headers.set("X-Robots-Tag", NOINDEX["X-Robots-Tag"]);
  response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
  return response;
}

export const config = {
  // Everything except route handlers, Next's own files and files with an
  // extension; and everything under /admin, files included, so no build
  // output of the review page can be fetched around the password.
  matcher: ["/((?!api|_next|_vercel|.*\\..*).*)", "/admin", "/admin/:path*"],
};
```

`src/app/admin/pool/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { computeCoverage } from "@/pipeline/report.ts";
import type { Flag, Pool, PoolPlayer } from "@/pipeline/types.ts";

// Jalel's review of the pool: coverage, flags, missing photos, and every
// footballer with the source of each field. English on purpose: a tool, not
// part of the game, so nothing here goes in messages/.

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Pool review",
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

async function loadPool(): Promise<Pool> {
  return JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "pool.json"), "utf8")) as Pool;
}

const percent = (count: number, total: number) => (total === 0 ? "0%" : `${Math.round((count * 100) / total)}%`);

function sources(player: PoolPlayer): string {
  return Object.entries(player.provenance)
    .map(([field, p]) => `${field}: ${p.source}${p.asOf ? ` (${p.asOf})` : ""}`)
    .join(" · ");
}

function born(p: PoolPlayer): string {
  if (p.governorate) return p.governorate;
  if (p.birthCountry && p.birthCountry !== "TN") return `abroad: ${p.birthCountry}`;
  return "unknown";
}

const cell = "border-b border-pitch-700 px-2 py-1 text-start align-top";

export default async function PoolReviewPage() {
  const pool = await loadPool();
  const clubs = new Map(pool.clubs.map((c) => [c.id, c.nameLatin]));
  const names = new Map(pool.players.map((p) => [p.wikidataId, p.nameLatin]));
  const kinds = new Map<string, Flag[]>();
  for (const flag of pool.flags) kinds.set(flag.kind, [...(kinds.get(flag.kind) ?? []), flag]);
  const noPhoto = pool.players.filter((p) => p.photo === null);
  const active = pool.players.filter((p) => p.pools.active).length;
  const legend = pool.players.filter((p) => p.pools.legend).length;

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-10">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Pool review</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-chalk-dim">
          {pool.players.length} footballers ({active} active, {legend} legends), {pool.clubs.length} clubs,{" "}
          {pool.honours.length} honours, {pool.flags.length} flags. This is data/pool.json as deployed.
        </p>
      </header>

      <table className="w-full max-w-2xl border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="mb-2 text-start text-xl font-semibold text-mint">Coverage</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th className={cell}>Field</th>
            <th className={cell}>Footballers</th>
            <th className={cell}>Share</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {computeCoverage(pool.players).map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.field}>
              <td className={cell}>{row.field}</td>
              <td className={cell}>
                {row.count} of {row.total}
              </td>
              <td className={cell}>{percent(row.count, row.total)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <section aria-labelledby="flags">
        <h2 id="flags" className="text-xl font-semibold text-mint">
          Flags
        </h2>
        {[...kinds].map(([kind, flags]) => (
          <details key={kind} className="mt-2">
            <summary className="cursor-pointer">
              {kind} ({flags.length})
            </summary>
            <ul className="ms-6 list-disc text-sm">
              {flags.map((f, index) => (
                <li key={`${f.subject}-${index}`}>
                  {names.get(f.subject) ?? f.subject} ({f.subject}): {f.detail}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </details>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="no-photo">
        <h2 id="no-photo" className="text-xl font-semibold text-mint">
          Without a photo ({noPhoto.length})
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-chalk-dim">{noPhoto.map((p) => p.nameLatin).join(", ") || "None."}</p>
      </section>

      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="mb-2 text-start text-xl font-semibold text-mint">Footballers</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            {["Footballer", "Position", "Club", "Caps / goals", "Born", "Pools", "Sources"].map((h) => (
              <th key={h} className={cell}>
                {h}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {pool.players.map((p) => (
            <tr key={p.id}>
              <td className={cell}>
                <span className="font-semibold">{p.nameLatin}</span>
                <br />
                <span lang="ar" dir="rtl">
                  {p.nameArabic ?? "—"}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className={cell}>
                {p.position}
                {p.positionDetail ? ` (${p.positionDetail})` : ""}
              </td>
              <td className={cell}>{p.clubId ? (clubs.get(p.clubId) ?? p.clubId) : "—"}</td>
              <td className={cell}>
                {p.caps} / {p.goals}
                {p.capsAsOf ? ` (${p.capsAsOf})` : ""}
              </td>
              <td className={cell}>
                {p.birthDate}, {born(p)}
              </td>
              <td className={cell}>{[p.pools.active && "active", p.pools.legend && "legend"].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}</td>
              <td className={cell}>{sources(p)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </main>
  );
}
```

## Strings Jalel will need to write (not in this sprint)

The Sources page and the photo credit (Sprint 2) need, in Derja Arabic script, Arabizi and French: the page title ("Sources"); one sentence on where the data comes from; "Photo: {author}, {licence}"; "Data: Wikipedia (CC BY-SA 4.0)" with "article" and "history" links; "Wikidata (CC0)"; "Results: martj42 (CC0)"; "Something wrong? Tell us" (the future report button). The governorate tile's "Abroad: {country}" also needs his wording.

## What Jalel does or supplies this sprint

- Confirms or changes the assumed decisions (Task 1 step 1), about 20 minutes.
- Reviews the data once (Task 11 step 1), about 2 hours: governorate names and regions, the Ligue 1 list, flagged conflicts, a 20-footballer spot check.
- Sets `ADMIN_PASSWORD` in Vercel; allows Actions to open pull requests (Task 11 step 2), about 5 minutes.
- Gives the go-ahead to merge and to start the first nightly run.
- From then on, reviews the nightly pull request when there is one, about 10 minutes.

## Self-review

- **Spec coverage (deliverable "nightly pipeline, first pool of about 300 with provenance, hidden /admin/pool"):** schema for every §11 gap (club history → `player_clubs`; honours → `honours`; aliases, `capsAsOf`, `positionDetail`, birthplace and birth country, wiki titles, structured photo credit on `players`; league, confederation, French name, `ligue1` on `clubs`; governorate → region via `governorates`) → Task 2, with SQL constraints tested and `docs/schema.md`. Wikidata Q1–Q3 with the title fix, plus aliases and Q4 → Task 5. MediaWiki en + fr, 50 titles, ≥ 5 s, Retry-After, maxlag, User-Agent → Tasks 3 and 4. Both infobox parsers with every trap from sources §3.1 → Task 4. Newer as-of wins, newer-but-lower flagged → Task 6. Commons licences → Task 3. martj42 (latest date checked first) → Tasks 1 and 3. Overrides reviewed by Jalel → Tasks 6 and 11. Merge with per-field provenance → Tasks 6 and 7. `data/pool.json` → Task 8. Sync into Postgres → Task 9. Probe with STOP rules → Task 1. Nightly workflow with a pull request, never merging, club changes flagged, last good cache → Tasks 7 (report) and 9. `/admin/pool` hidden, English, outside `messages/` → Task 10. Attribution data stored now, page later, argued → decision P22 and `data/README.md`. CI green per task; Lighthouse untouched; deploy and handover → Task 11.
- **Placeholders:** none in code. Values only a run can give (counts in two commit messages, revision ids in the fixtures README, the expected values read from the recorded articles) are produced by named steps.
- **Types:** `Infobox`, `WdPlayer`, `WdMembership`, `WdClub`, `WdHonour`, `Photo`, `Match`, `PoolPlayer`, `PoolClub`, `PoolHonour`, `Flag`, `Pool`, `SourceStatus` are defined once in Task 3 and used with the same names afterwards; `ClubIndex`/`buildClubIndex` (Task 6) feed `buildPool` (Task 7) and `run` (Task 8); `syncPool` (Task 9) maps every `PoolPlayer` field to the columns of Task 2; flag kinds in tests are members of `FlagKind`.
- **Known risks, each with a stop or a fallback:** Wikipedia 429 from a shared runner (cache, report says `cached`, Task 9; probe stops, Task 1); the French infobox shape (Task 1 step 8 stop before Task 4); Node type stripping (Task 3 step 3 stop); the clubs query with many titles (Task 8 step 5: time it, split it); the pool size (P19, Task 8 stop); Prisma refusing `migrate dev --create-only` non-interactively (Task 2 step 4 fallback); a pull request opened by the workflow token not starting CI (dispatch, Task 9); all pull requests share the one preview database (P10), so two open previews overwrite each other's pool, which is acceptable for a review copy.
